Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The logistic operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 153. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 428 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the logistic space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 153 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 428. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)
